## Not confirmed or not published

- YZ450F 2020-2022: the oil check bolt used for the level check has no published tightening torque; the guide says snug on a good washer and check for leaks.
- Two-stroke 2021-2027: the premix ratio prints as "30" (break-in "15") under "Premix ratio"; the illustration on the same page shows 30 : 1. The 2020 manuals print "Mixing ratio: 30:1" and "15:1".
- YZ250F 2020, YZ450F 2020 and all two-strokes: spring preload changes need the rear frame removed; the guide only states the standard preload and leaves the adjustment out.
- YZ250 2021 (BF1-2819U-10) covers YZ250 and YZ250X: the YZ250 column values are used (fork rebound 12, fork compression 12, shock rebound 10, fast compression 1 3/8 turns); slow compression 12 clicks and preload 12.0 mm are printed once for both models.
- YZ125 2020 and YZ250 2020 (old layout): suspension standard positions are printed per model (YZ125/YZ125X, YZ250/YZ250X); the YZ125 and YZ250 values are used. The shock rebound and compression standards are printed as ranges.
- Two-stroke 2020: rear brake pedal locknut torque not printed in the adjustment procedure; left out.
- Brake pad wear limits are given as wear-indicator descriptions (2021-2027) and as a 1.0 mm (0.04 in) lining limit (2020 two-strokes).
- YZ65 and YZ85 were not covered.
